Thus:

 Term B (r as j, i=r+k valid): -h_{r+k}
Sum if both valid: 1 - h_{r-k} - h_{r+k}
If only as i: 1 - h_{r-k}
If only as j: -h_{r+k}
If none: 0.

Thus single delta vector can be built with two segment additions:
arr = np.zeros(2n-1)
idx1 = off+p - np.arange(n)  # decreasing contiguous segment indices p? off+p to off+p-n+1
arr[idx1] += d*(1-h)
idx2 = off - off? off - p + np.arange(n) # contiguous increasing off-p to off-p+n-1
arr[idx2] -= d*h
Note both segments overlap in middle; adding works.

Potential exact local algorithm: At each step, given active set A and current h feasible, solve a linearized problem: minimize t? The first-order decrease for transferring mass between coordinates is determined by projected gradient on affine cone: For feasible transfer vector v with sum 0, 0≤h_i ±, the directional derivative for active average is ∇f_A · v. We want v that decreases while staying feasible. In box with sum, this is like choose i to decrease if ∇_i high at upper/interior, j to increase if ∇_j low. The best direction without step is set i argmax ∇_i among coordinates with headroom down, j argmin ∇_j among headroom up, if high>low. Then line search. This is what we can do.

Nonlinear exact objective for active average is bilinear. We can perform a backtracking line search along v = e_j - e_i with amount. Since v changes two coordinates, we can compute exact active objective along that line (linear + quadratic cross if relevant) and choose best feasible amt. For active A, c_k(amt) = c_k0 + amt * l_k + amt^2 * q_k where l_k from individual gradients, q_k = 1 if k=±(i-j) else 0 (raw). Then active average = base + amt*L + amt^2*Q (Q = (# active cross lags)/|A|). To minimize average active over [0, maxamt], the unconstrained minimizer if Q>0 is -L/(2Q); if Q=0, since linear, choose one endpoint depending sign. But we also must keep max active maybe, not average. But minimizing average is okay to reduce. We can choose candidate amt as min(maxamt, abs(L?) * step, etc. Then verify global.

This suggests efficient line search: For each pair, compute L = grad_j - grad_i (for active average). If L<0, reducing. Choose amt = min(maxamt, -eta*L?) But L is derivative in raw units. maxamt limited. We can test a few: max/4, max/2, max. Since objective along line convex? c_k linear plus possible positive quadratic (amt^2) for cross if active; max of线性/quadratic not necessarily convex but average active is convex if quadratic positive? Individual c_k may have positive quadratic; average too. So best either endpoint or -L/(2Q). We can try both. Then full c update.

We should also consider moving mass from an interior coordinate (not at bounds) can increase/decrease; line search can choose amt both directions. For coordinate descent, we can choose best i,j among top candidates.

Exact update function for move:
```
def apply_move(h, c_raw, i, j, amt):
    if amt==0: return h,c
    d_i=-amt; d_j=amt
    c_new = c_raw.copy()
    # add single delta i
    c_new[idx1_i] += d_i*(1-h)
    c_new[idx2_i] -= d_i*h  # since single formula second seg -= d*h
    # for j
    c_new[idx1_j] += d_j*(1-h)
    c_new[idx2_j] -= d_j*h
    # cross
    off=n-1
    if i != j:
       c_new[off+i-j] += amt*amt
       c_new[off+j-i] += amt*amt
    h_new = h.copy(); h_new[i]-=amt; h_new[j]+=amt; clip?
    return h_new,c_new
```
But note if updating c_raw in place, h should still old for deltas. Use old h for both segment values. If both segments overlap? Adding to c_new slices with old h fine. Need be careful if i/j segments overlap and c_new is view; no issue.

`weighted_grad(W, x, n)`:
```
M=2*n-1; off=n-1
cs = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(W)))
L = cs[n:2*n] - cs[0:n]
P1 = fast_convolve(x, W)[:2*n-1]  # length exactly
P1 = P1[off:off+n]
hr = x[::-1]
PW = fast_convolve(W, hr)[:2*n-1]
P2 = PW[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
return L - P1 - P2
```

Beatty threshold:
```
def make_beatty(n, slope, rng=None):
   t = np.arange(n)
   vals = np.mod(t * slope, 1.0)
   h = (vals < 0.5).astype(float)
   # adjust exact count: if sum > n/2, change largest vals among ones to 0? etc.
   h = project_feasible(h,n)
   return h
```
For slope = (sqrt(5)+1)/2 * 0.5? Need ensure balanced. If slope irrational and threshold 0.5, sum approx n/2. Project fixes. Good. Use slopes: 0.618, 0.707, 0.414, 0.5? (0.5 rational not). slope golden conjugate = (sqrt5-1)/2 ~0.618. Also sqrt2/2 ~0.707.
```
def make_sinusoid(n, freq, amp):
   x=np.arange(n)
   h=0.5 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x/n)
   h = project_feasible(np.clip(h,0,1),n)
```
freq 1..6, amp=0.5 or 0.4. If amp=0.5 sinus gives [0,1], sum maybe n/2 exactly for integer freq? sin sum zero; sum =n/2. Good.

def resample_fn(h_old, n_new):
   old_n=len(h_old); x_old=np.arange(old_n); y=h_old
   x_new=np.linspace(0,old_n,n_new,endpoint=False) # preserve positions
   h_new=np.interp(x_new, x_old, np.concatenate([h_old, [h_old[0]]])) # piecewise linear not step
   project
```

```
def local_mass_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_no_improve=50):
    h = project_feasible(h,n)
    c = compute_c5_raw(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.max(); dx=2/n; best_dx=best_c*dx
    no_improve=0
    iter=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
       c = compute_c5_raw(best_h,n)? maintain; use current.
       raw_max = c.max()
       # active selection
       rank = 25 maybe dynamic
       # choose active as indices of top min(rank, count within tol)
       vals, idxs = np.sort? For M up to 1000, use np.argpartition.
       if raw_max <=0: break
       tol = max(1e-9, 0.002*raw_max)
       cand = np.where(c >= raw_max - tol)[0]
       if len(cand) > rank: take top rank by c
       elif len(cand)==0: cand=np.argsort(-c)[:rank]
       W=np.zeros(M); W[cand]=1/len(cand)
       grad=weighted_grad(W,best_h,n)
       # candidate pairs
       down_mask = best_h > 1e-8
       up_mask = best_h < 1.0 - 1e-8
       if not down_mask.any() or not up_mask.any(): break
       down_idx = np.where(down_mask)[0]
       up_idx = np.where(up_mask)[0]
       # sort by grad
       g_down = grad[down_idx]; order_d = down_idx[np.argsort(-g_down)]
       g_up = grad[up_idx]; order_u = up_idx[np.argsort(g_up)]
       Mset_top = 12 if n<=600 else 8
       top_pairs=[]
       for i in order_d[:Mset_top]:
          for j in order_u[:Mset_top]:
             if i!=j: top_pairs.append((i,j))
       # some random pairs
       rnd_pairs=[]
       for _ in range(20):
          i=down_idx[rng.randint(len(down_idx))]
          j=up_idx[rng.randint(len(up_idx))]
          if i!=j: rnd_pairs.append((i,j))
       # also consider reverse? down high gradient decrease, up low increase. What if best move decreases low and increases high? That increases. But maybe due nonlinear cross? Ignore.
       # candidate improvements: track best
       best_gain=1e-15
       best_move=None
       tested=0
       for i,j in top_pairs + rnd_pairs:
          if time > deadline: break
          maxamt = min(best_h[i], 1-best_h[j])
          if maxamt < 1e-10: continue
          L = grad[j] - grad[i]
          # allow if L < 0 or (maybe if i-j active cross and positive quadratic? skip)
          if L >= -1e-12:
             # test only random small? skip
             continue
          # generate amts
          amts = {maxamt, 0.5*maxamt, 0.25*maxamt, 0.1*maxamt, 0.02*maxamt}
          # add small baseline np.clip
          # add exact average minimizer:
          off=n-1; ki=abs(i-j); k_i=off+ i-j; k_j=off+j-i
          qcnt = (k_i in cand?) + (k_j in cand?) if k_i != k_j else 1
          # Note cand can contain duplicates? no.
          if qcnt>0:
             q = qcnt / len(cand)
             if L < 0:
                aopt = -L/(2*q)
                if 1e-8 < aopt < maxamt: amts.add(aopt)
          for amt in amts:
             amt = min(amt, maxamt)
             if amt < 1e-10: continue
             c_new, h_new = apply_move_copy(best_h,c,i,j,amt,n)
             cmax = c_new.max()
             if cmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_c=cmax; best_h=h_new; best_move=(i,j,amt); improve=True
                break # for this pair
          # after pair if improve, maybe restart? We can update and break loop to recompute grad.
       if best_move:
          # recompute c to be safe
          c = compute_c5_raw(best_h,n)
          # if c.max() < best_c? update; else use previous
       else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve > 30:
             # random perturbation: do several random small transfers that don't worsen too much? or break after maybe noise+optimize
             # Try accepting a small worsening to escape: up to 50 random moves, choose least worsening <= raw_max*1.005; apply if time.
             ...
             if still no: break
```

def apply_move_copy(h,c,i,j,amt,n):
    cnew = c.copy()
    hnew = h.copy()
    off=n-1
    d=-amt
    cnew[off+i - np.arange(n)?] # use slice to avoid array allocation? Need values (1-h) and h length n. Slices:
    seg_a_i = slice(off+i, off+i-n, -1)
    cnew[seg_a_i] += d * (1.0 - h)
    seg_b_i = slice(off-i, off-i+n, 1)
    cnew[seg_b_i] -= d * h
    d=amt
    cnew[off+j...]+=...
    cnew[off-i? for j]
    cnew[off+i-j] += amt*amt
    cnew[off+j-i] += amt*amt
    hnew[i] = max(0,h[i]-amt); hnew[j]=min(1,h[j]+amt)
    return hnew,cnew
```

Project_feasible from previous: It clips and iterates additive/multiplicative. Need ensure if h sum target exact. For n not integer target? n even, target integer. Code:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target=n/2.0
    h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
    for _ in range(10000):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1-h; tot=room.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += diff/tot*room
       else:
          tot=h.sum()
          if tot<1e-14: break
          h += diff/tot*h
    return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This additive for positive diff adds proportionally to room; if diff large, may exceed? It adds full diff distributed over room, at most h+room=1, good. For negative, multiplicative reduction. Converges. Good.

But if h contains NaN? check. Use `if not np.all(np.isfinite(h))` fallback.

So sparse gradient:
```
def weighted_grad_sparse(W, x, n):
   g=np.zeros(n)
   nonzero = np.nonzero(W)[0]
   for t in nonzero:
      w=W[t]; k=t-(n-1)
      if k>=0:
         g[k:n] += w*(1-x[:n-k])
         g[:n-k] += -w*x[k:n]
      else:
         q=-k
         g[:n-q] += w*(1-x[q:n])
         g[q:n] += -w*x[:n-q]
   return g
```
Ensure lengths: for k>=0, 1-x[:n-k] length n-k; g[k:n] length n-k. second g[:n-k] length n-k; -w*x[k:n] length n-k. For k<0 q>0: 1-x[q:n] length n-q; g[:n-q]; -w*x[:n-q] length n-q; g[q:n]. Good.
This is O(K*n) Python loops but vector segments. For K=20, 20*500=10k, faster than convolve. For full softmax K~999 dense, use convolve. We can choose if nnz < n//2 use sparse else dense. For smooth full, use dense. For active top K, use sparse. Good. Need pass W maybe dense with zeros. `weighted_grad(W,x,n)` can check nnz = np.count_nonzero(W); if nnz < max(20, n//3?) use sparse; else dense. But count_nonzero O(M) small.

def active_obj(x, n, active, mu):
   c=compute_c5_raw(x,n); val=float(c[active].mean())
   if mu>0: val += mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)**2
   return val
def active_grad(x,n,active,mu):
   g=active_uniform_grad(active,x,n)
   if mu: g += 2*mu*(x.sum()-0.5*n)
   return g
```

Perturbation function:
```
def perturb_h(h,n,rng,strength=0.05):
   h=h.copy()
   k=int(n*0.2)

Now, top indices selection:
```
def select_active(c, top_k, frac_tol=0.002):
   M=len(c); maxv=c.max()
   cand=np.where(c >= maxv - max(1e-12, frac_tol*maxv))[0]
   if len(cand) > top_k:
      # choose top top_k among cand by c
      part = np.argpartition(-c[cand], top_k)[:top_k]
      return cand[part]
   elif len(cand)<top_k:
      # fill with next highest overall
      if len(cand) == 0: return np.argsort(-c)[:top_k]
      need=top_k-len(cand)
      # get top overall not in cand? simple use argsort(-c)[:top_k]
      all_top=np.argsort(-c)[:top_k]
      return all_top
   return cand
```
`np.argsort(-c)` M~1000 fine.

Pseudo:
```
def local_mass_search(h, n, deadline, rng, min_time_s=0):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5_raw(h,n)
    best_h = h.copy(); best_craw = c.max(); best_c = best_craw*2/n
    off = n-1
    move_count=0
    no_improve=0
    while time.time() < deadline - 5:
        c = compute_c5_raw(best_h,n)
        raw_max = c.max()
        active = select_active(c, 32, 0.002)
        w = 1.0/len(active)
        grad = active_uniform_grad(active, best_h, n)
        down_idx = np.where(best_h > 1e-7)[0]
        up_idx = np.where(best_h < 1.0 - 1e-7)[0]
        if len(down_idx)==0 or len(up_idx)==0: break
        gd = grad[down_idx]
        gu = grad[up_idx]
        top_d = down_idx[np.argsort(-gd)]
        top_u = up_idx[np.argsort(gu)]
        md = 10 if n<=600 else 6
        pairs=[]
        for a in top_d[:md]:
          for b in top_u[:md]:
            if a!=b: pairs.append((a,b))
        # random pairs
        for _ in range(15):
          i=down_idx[rng.randint(len(down_idx))]
          j=up_idx[rng.randint(len(up_idx))]
          if i!=j: pairs.append((i,j))
        improved=False
        # maybe sort pairs by grad diff
        # We'll test in order of largest grad_i - grad_j
        scored=[]
        for i,j in set(pairs): # avoid duplicates but set of tuples overhead; use list and maybe dict
           score=grad[i]-grad[j]
           if score > 0: scored.append((-score,i,j))
        scored.sort()
        for _,i,j in scored[:80]:
           if time.time() > deadline: break
           maxamt = min(best_h[i], 1.0 - best_h[j])
           if maxamt < 1e-9: continue
           # if best_h[i] close 0 or best_h[j] close 1? maxamt ok.
           amts=[maxamt, 0.5*maxamt, 0.25*maxamt, 0.1*maxamt, 0.02*maxamt]
           # add small
           amts.append(min(0.001, maxamt*0.5) if maxamt>1e-6 else maxamt)
           # exact active average optimum
           qcnt=0
           ki=off + i - j
           kj=off + j - i
           if ki in active_set: qcnt+=1
           if kj in active_set and kj != ki: qcnt+=1
           # if lag0 active, q for self? Use active_set contains off? q for average includes -2 for self at lag0 plus cross maybe. Let's compute q for active average:
           q = qcnt / len(active)
           if off in active_set: q -= 2.0/len(active)
           if q > 0:
              L = grad[j] - grad[i]
              if L < 0:
                 aopt = -L/(2*q)
                 if 1e-8 < aopt < maxamt: amts.append(aopt)
          for amt in amts:
             if amt < 1e-9 or amt > maxamt: continue
             hnew,cnew = apply_move_copy(best_h,c,i,j,amt,n)
             # If clipped? apply returns exact maybe not project. Check bounds.
             if hnew.min() < -1e-10 or hnew.max() > 1+1e-10:
                hnew=project_feasible(hnew,n)
                cnew=compute_c5_raw(hnew,n)
             cmax=cnew.max()
             if cmax < best_c - 1e-12:
                best_h=hnew; best_c=cmax
                c=cnew
                improved=True
                move_count +=1
                if move_count % 25 ==0:
                   best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
                   c=compute_c5_raw(best_h,n)
                   best_c=c.max()
                break
          if improved:
             # recompute grad? break out of pairs and outer while
             no_improve=0
             break
        if not improved:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve == 5:
              # random escape: try 100 random small transfers, accept best even if slightly worse? 
              c, best_h, best_c = random_escape(best_h,c,best_c,n,rng,deadline)
              if c.max() >= best_c - 1e-15? need update
              no_improve=0
              if time.time() > deadline - 20: break
           elif no_improve > 20:
              break
```

`apply_move_c(c,h,i,j,amt,n)`:
```
cnew=c.copy()
off=n-1; a=amt
# i d=-a
if i>0: sa=slice(off+i, i-1, -1) else slice(off+i, None, -1)
cnew[sa] += -a * (1.0 - h)
cnew[slice(off-i, off-i+n, 1)] -= -a * h  # as linear: cnew[seg_b] -= d*h, d=-a -> += a*h
# for d=-a, second term is -d*h = +a*h. We can write generic d to avoid mistakes.
```
Let's write generic with d to reduce bug:
```
def add_single_linear(cnew, h, p, d, n):
    off=n-1
    if p>0:
       cnew[slice(off+p, p-1, -1)] += d * (1.0 - h)
    else:
       cnew[slice(off+p, None, -1)] += d * (1.0 - h)
    cnew[slice(off-p, off-p+n, 1)] -= d * h
```
No closures allowed? This is a helper top-level; call. But function call per segment per candidate maybe overhead. Could inline in apply_move_c. But top-level helper okay, not closure. However for speed, inline.

Let's carefully derive signs:
For coordinate p with change d, linear delta (ignoring self) = A: d*(1-h) on as-i segment; B: -d*h on as-j segment.
In `cnew[seg_b] -= d * h`: yes.
For i d=-amt: seg_a += -amt*(1-h); seg_b -= -amt*h = +amt*h.
For j d=+amt: seg_a += amt*(1-h); seg_b -= amt*h.
Then self: cnew[off] -= 2*amt*amt.
Cross: cnew[off+i-j] += amt*amt; cnew[off+j-i] += amt*amt.

Let's test n=2 move again with this code:
i=0,d=-1: seg_a slice(1,None,-1) [1,0] += -1*(1-h) = [0,1]? 1-h=[0,1], - => [0,-1], add to indices [1,0]: c [0? start [1,0,0] -> idx1 +=0, idx0 +=-1 => [0,0,0]. seg_b slice(1,3) idx[1,2] -= -1*h = +h [1,0] => [1,0,0]? Wait c after i only should [0,0,0]? For h new [0,0] c all 0. Starting [1,0,0], i only changes h0 1->0, h1 0: expected [0,0,0]. Our after seg_a [0,0,0]? Let's recalc: start [1,0,0]. seg_a: indices [1,0], values -1*(1-h) = -[0,1]=[0,-1]. c[1]+=0 ->0, c[0]+=-1 ->0. Now [0,0,0]. seg_b: indices [1,2], values? `cnew[seg_b] -= d*h` with d=-1 => -= -1*h = +h=[1,0]. c[1]+=1 ->1, c[2]+=0. Now [0,1,0]. That suggests linear delta for i alone central +1. But exact i alone should central 0; missing self -1. Then self after j will subtract 2 at off, making central -1 after both? Let's continue j=1,d=+1. seg_a slice(2,0,-1) indices [2,1], vals +(1-h)=[0,1]; c[2]+=0, c[1]+=1 -> [0,2,0]. seg_b slice(0,2) indices[0,1], vals -= +1*h=[1,0]; c[0]-=1 -> -1, c[1]-=0 -> [ -1,2,0]. cross idx off+i-j=0: +1 -> [0,2,0]; idx2 +1 -> [0,2,1]; self off=1 subtract 2 -> [0,0,1]. Central correct. i alone central linear +1 self -1. Good. cnew during candidate after all moves exact. We only check final cnew, so fine. Intermediate negative values no issue.

But note `cnew[seg_a_i] += d*(1-h)` uses vector (1-h) length n; for seg_a_i decreasing, the order of values must correspond to decreasing indices. For p=0, seg indices [off,...,0], as i j=p-k =? For p=0, r coordinate p? Wait this update is for c vector when changing h_p. We derived using position p, not coordinate r. For single coordinate p, delta c[t] as a vector over t: as i: c index off+p-j for j=0..n-1, values d*(1-h_j). This vector over j increasing is [d(1-h0), d(1-h1), ...]. But c indices as j increases: off+p, off+p-1,... decreasing. So assigning `cnew[slice(start,stop,-1)] += d*(1-h)` works because slice decreasing pairs first element of h (j=0) with index off+p, second with off+p-1. Good.
As j: c index off-p+i for i=0..n-1, values -d*h_i, i increasing, indices increasing. `cnew[slice(off-p, off-p+n)] -= d*h` works. Good.

`lbfgs_step`: We need pass args. Since objective/grad functions have signature (x,n,...). For smooth: args=(n,alpha,mu), grad args same. For active: args=(n,active,mu), grad args same. We can write:
```
def lbfgs_step(fun, jac, x0, n, args, maxit, deadline):
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    options={'maxiter': maxit, 'maxfun': max(1, int(maxit*6)), 'ftol':1e-20, 'gtol':1e-14, 'maxcor':50, 'maxls':40}
    try:
       res=minimize(fun, x0, jac=jac, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options=options)
       x=res.x
    except Exception:
       x=x0
    return project_feasible(x,n)
```
Note if L-BFGS raises mid, may not have res. Catch. If time over, not aborted.

def polish_solution(h,n,deadline,max_seconds):

    h=project_feasible(h,n); best_h=h; best_c=compute_c5(h,n)

    mus=[1e-4,1e-4,1e-3,1e-3,2e-3]

       rem=max(10, limit - time.time())

    # active

`resample_parent`: If parent is None. If old_n=1? handle.
```
def resample_parent(h_old,n):
    old_n = len(h_old)
    if old_n == 1:
       h = np.full(n, h_old[0]); return project_feasible(h,n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, old_n, old_n, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, old_n, n, endpoint=False)
    h_ext = np.concatenate((h_old, h_old[:1]))
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_ext), n)
```

Now, `perturb_h`: we need feasible after. Use random transfers preserving sum:
```
def perturb_h(h,n,rng,strength):
    h = h.copy()
    target = 0.5*n
    n_moves = max(10, n//5)
    for _ in range(n_moves):
       i = rng.randint(n); j=rng.randint(n)
       if i==j: continue
       if h[i] > 1e-6:
          amt = rng.uniform(0.0, strength)
          if amt > h[i]: amt=h[i]
          if h[j]+amt <=1: h[i]-=amt; h[j]+=amt
    return project_feasible(h,n)
```
This may not change much if strength small. Good.

Now, `local_mass_search` with `max_seconds`. It should return best_h (feasible) and c. We need be careful with `c` variable raw. Use `dx` for c_bound. In loop, `cur_h, cur_craw` current. `best_h, best_craw` global best. At end return best_h, best_craw*2/n.

Pseudo code no lambdas:
```
def local_mass_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_seconds=60):
    t0=time.time(); limit=min(deadline, t0+max_seconds)
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    cur_h=h.copy(); cur_c=compute_c_raw(cur_h,n)
    best_h=cur_h.copy(); best_craw=cur_c.max()
    if not np.isfinite(best_craw) or best_craw <0: ...
    off=n-1
    move_count=0; no_improve=0
    max_no=20
    while time.time() < limit - 5:
        cmax=cur_c.max()
        if time.time() > limit: break
        active=select_active(cur_c, 40, 0.002)
        if len(active)==0: break
        active_set=set(int(t) for t in active)  # set comprehension has lambda? no comprehension okay? It is not lambda; but use list + set? Could use `active_set = set(active.tolist())`.
        grad=active_uniform_grad(active, cur_h, n)
        down = np.where(cur_h > 1e-7)[0]
        up = np.where(cur_h < 1.0 - 1e-7)[0]
        if len(down)==0 or len(up)==0: break
        gd=grad[down]; gu=grad[up]
        top_d=down[np.argsort(-gd)][:12]
        top_u=up[np.argsort(gu)][:12]
        pairs=[]; seen=set()
        for a in top_d:
          for b in top_u:
            if a != b:
              tkey=(int(a),int(b))
              if tkey not in seen: seen.add(tkey); pairs.append(tkey)
        # add random 20
        for _ in range(20):
           a=int(down[rng.randint(len(down))]); b=int(up[rng.randint(len(up))])
           if a!=b:
             tkey=(a,b)
             if tkey not in seen: seen.add(tkey); pairs.append(tkey)
        # score pairs
        scored=[]
        for (i,j) in pairs:
            s=grad[i]-grad[j]
            if s > 1e-10:
                scored.append((-s, i, j))
        scored.sort()
        improved=False
        max_test = min(len(scored), 100)
        mults = [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002,0.001,0.0005,0.0002]
        for idx in range(max_test):
           if time.time() > limit: break
           _, i, j = scored[idx]
           maxamt = cur_h[i]
           room = 1.0 - cur_h[j]
           if room < maxamt: maxamt=room
           if maxamt < 1e-10: continue
           # amts unique
           amt_candidates=[]
           for m in mults:
              amt=maxamt*m
              if amt < 1e-11: continue
              # avoid duplicate
              if not amt_candidates or abs(amt-amt_candidates[-1])>1e-14: amt_candidates.append(amt)
           # add aopt
           qcnt=0
           ki=off+i-j; kj=off+j-i
           if ki in active_set: qcnt +=1
           if kj in active_set and kj != ki: qcnt +=1
           q = qcnt / float(len(active))
           if off in active_set: q -= 2.0/float(len(active))
           if q > 1e-14:
              L = grad[j] - grad[i]
              if L < -1e-12:
                aopt = -L/(2.0*q)
                if aopt < maxamt and aopt > 1e-10:
                   amt_candidates.append(aopt)
           for amt in amt_candidates:
              if amt > maxamt: amt = maxamt
              if amt < 1e-10: continue
              cnew = apply_move_c(cur_c, cur_h, i, j, amt, n)
              newmax = float(cnew.max())
              if newmax < best_craw - 1e-12:
                  hnew=apply_move_h(cur_h,i,j,amt,n)
                  # check bounds; clip tiny
                  if hnew.min() < -1e-9 or hnew.max() > 1+1e-9:
                     hnew=project_feasible(hnew,n)
                     cnew=compute_c_raw(hnew,n)
                     newmax=float(cnew.max())
                  # if newmax still improved
                  if newmax < best_craw:
                     best_craw=newmax; best_h=hnew.copy()
                     cur_h=hnew.copy(); cur_c=cnew.copy()
                     improved=True; move_count+=1
                     if move_count % 25 == 0:
                         cur_h=project_feasible(cur_h,n)
                         cur_c=compute_c_raw(cur_h,n)
                         cm=cur_c.max()
                         if cm < best_craw: best_craw=cm
                     break
              # if no improve, continue amt
           if improved:
              break
        if improved:
           no_improve=0
        else:
           no_improve +=1
           if no_improve > 15:
              break
    # final project best
    best_h=project_feasible(best_h,n)
    best_c=compute_c5(best_h,n)
    return best_h, best_c
```

`weighted_grad_dense`: If W sum zero? no. Use `W = np.asarray(W, dtype=float)`. If `np.count_nonzero(W) == 0`, return zeros. For dense, if no nonzero? no.
```
def weighted_grad_dense(W, x, n):
    W = np.asarray(W, dtype=float)
    if W.size == 0: return np.zeros(n)
    cs = np.empty(W.size+1); cs[0]=0; cs[1:]=np.cumsum(W)
    L = cs[n:2*n] - cs[0:n]
    conv1 = fast_convolve(x, W)
    P1 = conv1[n-1:2*n-1]
    conv2 = fast_convolve(W, x[::-1])
    P2 = conv2[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return L - P1 - P2
```
Wait conv length = len(x)+len(W)-1 = n+(2n-1)-1 = 3n-2. We need P1 indices n-1 through 2n-2 inclusive? Off = n-1; length n => indices n-1..n+n-2 = 2n-3? Wait earlier I said off:off+n where off=n-1 => n-1..2n-2 inclusive? off+n = 2n-1 exclusive, last 2n-2. Yes length n. For n=5: indices 4..8. conv length 13 indices 0..12. OK. So slice `[n-1:2*n-1]` (exclusive 9 for n=5 -> 4..8). Good.
P2 same slice reversed. L cs length 2n? W size 9, cs size10. `cs[n:2*n]` indices 5..9 length5, `cs[0:n]` 0..4, good.

But if using FFT convolve, output length maybe exactly 3n-2. Good.

```
def select_active(c, top_k, frac_tol=0.002):
    M=len(c)
    if top_k >= M: return np.arange(M)
    maxv=c.max()
    tol=max(1e-12, frac_tol*maxv)
    cand = np.where(c >= maxv - tol)[0]
    if cand.size <= top_k:
       if cand.size == 0: return np.argsort(-c)[:top_k]
       # If fewer than top_k, fill with highest overall not already? Simpler return argsort top top_k
       return np.argsort(-c)[:top_k]
    else:
       # choose top top_k from cand
       order = np.argsort(-c[cand], kind='mergesort')
       return cand[order[:top_k]]
```
If cand has few but not all top? It returns overall top top_k, maybe includes cand and others. Good.

```
def select_active(c, top_k, frac_tol=1e-4, fill=True):
    M=len(c); maxv=c.max(); tol=max(1e-12, frac_tol*maxv)
    cand=np.where(c >= maxv - tol)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
       return np.argsort(-c)[:min(top_k,M)]
    if cand.size <= top_k:
       if fill:
          if cand.size < top_k:
             # add highest others not in cand? Use all top
             all_top=np.argsort(-c)[:top_k]
             if len(all_top) > cand.size:
                # union? Simple return all_top if it includes cand? all_top includes top cands because they are highest.
                return all_top
             # if all_top subset cand? return cand
          return cand
       else: return cand
    # > top_k choose top top_k among cand
    order=np.argsort(-c[cand])
    return cand[order[:top_k]]
```
For local, `fill=False` to keep only near max. For active LBFGS, `fill=True` top_k maybe. Use local `active=select_active(cur_c, 40, 1e-4, False)`. If cand size <40, active only those. If no cand (should), top fill.

```
def apply_move_h(h,i,j,amt,n):
   hnew=h.copy()
   hnew[i]-=amt; hnew[j]+=amt
   if hnew[i] < 0.0:
      if hnew[i] > -1e-12: hnew[i]=0.0
   if hnew[j] > 1.0:
      if hnew[j] < 1.0+1e-12: hnew[j]=1.0
   # if still out, clip
   if hnew[i] < 0.0: hnew[i]=0.0
   if hnew[j] > 1.0: hnew[j]=1.0
   return hnew
```

diff = target - h.sum()
if abs(diff) > 0:
   if diff >0:
      idx=np.argmax(1-h); h[idx]+=diff
   else:

But if diff not tiny, clipping. Project made diff tiny. We can add `enforce_sum(h,n)` top-level after final.
```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
   h=np.clip(h,0,1).copy()
   target=0.5*n
   for _ in range(10):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
      if diff>0:
         idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
         add=min(diff,1-h[idx])
         h[idx]+=add
      else:
         idx=int(np.argmax(h))
         add=min(-diff,h[idx])
         h[idx]-=add
   return h
```

    # local

       t_loc= min(220.0, max(20.0, rem*0.4))

    # second continuous after local

       rem=deadline-time.time()
       t_pol= min(120.0, max(20.0, rem*0.5))

       rem=deadline-time.time()
       t_loc= min(150.0, max(10.0, rem*0.7))
       h,c=local_mass_search(best_h,main_n,deadline,rng,t_loc)

    while time.time() < deadline - 120 and restarts < 3:

           if c<best_c: update
       restarts +=1
    # alternative n if time
    if time.time() < deadline - 160 and main_n*2 <= 1200:

       if c<best_c: update
    # final enforce and return

Detailed function implementations:

```
def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    try:
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        h = np.full(n, 0.5)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size == 0:
            h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            h = np.resize(h, n)
    for _ in range(1000):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / s) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
```
Using 1000 enough? Previous 10000. Use 2000 maybe. diff tiny after few. OK.

`enforce_sum`:
```
def enforce_sum(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,dtype=float),0,1).copy()
    target=0.5*float(n)
    for _ in range(100):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-14: return h
        if diff>0:
            idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
            add=min(diff, max(0.0,1.0-h[idx]))
            if add <=0: break
            h[idx]+=add
        else:
            idx=int(np.argmax(h))
            sub=min(-diff, h[idx])
            if sub <=0: break
            h[idx]-=sub
    return h
```
If h all 1 or 0 and infeasible, can't fix. But project ensured.

`correlate_exact`:
```
def correlate_exact(h,n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')
def compute_c5(h,n):
    raw=correlate_exact(h,n)
    c=float(np.max(raw))
    if c < 0 and c > -1e-12: c=0.0
    return c * (2.0/float(n))
```
For n=0? no.

`correlate_fast`:
```
def correlate_fast(h,n):
    if fftconvolve is not None and n > 800:
        return np.asarray(fftconvolve(h, (1.0-h)[::-1]), dtype=float)
    return correlate_exact(h,n)
```
Need if `fftconvolve` not defined? We set None.

`fast_convolve`:
```
def fast_convolve(a,b):
    la=len(a); lb=len(b)
    if fftconvolve is not None and (la + lb) > 2048:
        return np.asarray(fftconvolve(a,b), dtype=float)
    return np.convolve(a,b,mode='full')
```

`smooth_value`:
```
def smooth_value(c, alpha, x, n, mu):
    m=float(np.max(c))
    s=alpha*(c-m)
    smax=float(np.max(s))
    if smax > 400.0:
        sh=s-smax
        val=m + (smax + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(sh))))) / alpha
    else:
        val=m + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s)))) / alpha
    if mu > 0.0:
        diff=float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5*float(n)
        val += mu*diff*diff
    return float(val)
```
If exp(s) underflows when smax≤400 but s min -large; sum exp maybe 0? `lbfgs_step`:
```
def lbfgs_step(fun, jac, x0, n, args, maxit, limit):
    x0=np.clip(x0,0,1).copy()
    if maxit <=0: return x0
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    maxfun=max(1, int(maxit*8))
    options={'maxiter':int(maxit),'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-20,'gtol':1e-14,'maxcor':50,'maxls':30}
    try:
       res=minimize(fun,x0,jac=jac,args=args,method='L-BFGS-B',bounds=bounds,options=options)
       x=res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else x0
    except Exception:
       x=x0
    return project_feasible(x,n)
```
Need if `limit` not used? We don't early abort. Could ignore. But if maxit too high may overshoot. We estimate.

Maybe add if time in options? no.

`polish_solution`:
```
def polish_solution(h,n,deadline,max_seconds):
    t0=time.time()
    limit=min(float(deadline), t0+float(max_seconds))
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=compute_c5(h,n)
    if time.time() > limit: return best_h,best_c
    alphas=[5.0,20.0,100.0,500.0,2000.0]
    mus=[0.1,0.3,1.0,1.0,2.0]
    for idx in range(len(alphas)):
        if time.time() >= limit - 10: break
        alpha=alphas[idx]; mu=mus[idx] if idx < len(mus) else 2.0
        rem=limit-time.time()
        scale = 500.0 / float(n)
        if scale < 0.25: scale=0.25
        maxit=int(max(40, min(1500, rem*1.5*scale)))
        if maxit < 20: break
        x=lbfgs_step(smooth_full_obj, smooth_full_grad, best_h, n, (n,alpha,mu), maxit, limit)
        c=compute_c5(x,n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
    topks=[8,16,32]
    for topk in topks:
        for rr in range(2):
            if time.time() >= limit - 8: break
            if n > 1:
                cvec=correlate_exact(best_h,n)
                active=select_active(cvec, topk, 0.002, True)
                if len(active)>0:
                    rem=limit-time.time()
                    scale=500.0/float(n)
                    if scale<0.25: scale=0.25
                    maxit=int(max(20, min(1000, rem*1.0*scale)))
                    x=lbfgs_step(active_obj, active_grad, best_h, n, (n, active, 1.0), maxit, limit)
                    c=compute_c5(x,n)
                    if c<best_c: best_c=c; best_h=x.copy()
    return best_h,best_c
```
If max_seconds large but L-BFGS time under, it will do all. OK.

`select_active`:
```
def select_active(c, top_k, frac_tol, fill):
    M=len(c)
    kk=min(int(top_k), M)
    if kk <=0: return np.array([], dtype=int)
    maxv=float(np.max(c))
    tol=max(1e-12, abs(frac_tol)*max(0.0,maxv))
    cand=np.where(c >= maxv - tol)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
        return np.argsort(-c)[:kk]
    if cand.size <= kk:
        if fill and cand.size < kk:
            all_top=np.argsort(-c)[:kk]
            if all_top.size > cand.size:
                return all_top
        return cand
    order=np.argsort(-c[cand])
    return cand[order[:kk]]
```
If c max negative? tol fine. If maxv=0, tol=1e-12; cand all zero? if c all zero (impossible with mass?) works.

Now, `apply_move_c`: Need use n to compute slices. Let's code carefully:
```
def apply_move_c(c, h, i, j, amt, n):
    cnew = c.copy()
    off = n - 1
    # i change -amt
    d = -amt
    if i > 0:
        cnew[slice(off + i, i - 1, -1)] += d * (1.0 - h)
    else:
        cnew[slice(off + i, None, -1)] += d * (1.0 - h)
    cnew[slice(off - i, off - i + n, 1)] -= d * h
    # j change +amt
    d = amt
    if j > 0:
        cnew[slice(off + j, j - 1, -1)] += d * (1.0 - h)
    else:
        cnew[slice(off + j, None, -1)] += d * (1.0 - h)
    cnew[slice(off - j, off - j + n, 1)] -= d * h
    cnew[off + i - j] += amt * amt
    cnew[off + j - i] += amt * amt
    cnew[off] -= 2.0 * amt * amt
    return cnew
```
Check slice for i=0: `slice(off+0, None, -1)` works. For i>0 stop i-1. If i=1 stop 0; slice(off+1,0,-1) includes down to1. Good. For off-i+n stop maybe >? off-i+n = 2n-1 - i? For i=0 stop N? length 2n-1, stop 2n-1 valid (len). For i=n-1 stop off-(n-1)+n = n? Wait off=n-1, i=n-1 => stop = (n-1)-(n-1)+n = n. slice(0,n,1) indices 0..n-1. But as-j segment for p=n-1 should indices off-p=0 to off-p+n-1=n-1 length n. correct.
As-i segment for p=n-1: start off+p=2n-2, stop p-1=n-2, slice(2n-2,n-2,-1) indices 2n-2 down to n-1 length n. correct.

def apply_move_h(h,i,j,amt,n):
    hnew=h.copy()
    hnew[i] -= amt
    hnew[j] += amt

    if hnew[j] > 1.0:

       else: hnew[j]=1.0
    return hnew
```

def make_binary_from_values(vals,n):
    vals=np.asarray(vals,dtype=float)
    if vals.size != n: vals=np.resize(vals,n)
    k=n//2
    h=np.zeros(n)
    if k <=0: return project_feasible(h,n)
    if k >= n: h[:]=1; return project_feasible(h,n)
    idx=np.argpartition(vals, k-1)
    h[idx[-k:]] = 1.0

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

try:
    from scipy.signal import fftconvolve
except Exception:
    fftconvolve = None

AMT_MULTS = (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005, 0.002, 0.001, 0.0005, 0.0002)


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    try:
        h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        h = np.full(n, 0.5)
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size == 0:
            h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            h = np.resize(h, n)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    for _ in range(2000):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            tot = float(room.sum())
            if tot < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            if s < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff / s) * h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def enforce_sum(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        if h.size == 0:
            h = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            h = np.resize(h, n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    for _ in range(200):
        diff = target - float(h.sum())
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            add = min(diff, max(0.0, 1.0 - h[idx]))
            if add <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += add
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(h))
            sub = min(-diff, max(0.0, h[idx]))
            if sub <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] -= sub
    return np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)


def correlate_exact(h, n):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def compute_c5(h, n):
    raw = correlate_exact(h, n)
    c = float(np.max(raw))
    if c < 0.0 and c > -1e-12:
        c = 0.0
    return c * (2.0 / float(n))


def correlate_fast(h, n):
    if fftconvolve is not None and n > 800:
        out = fftconvolve(h, (1.0 - h)[::-1])
        return np.asarray(np.real(out), dtype=float)
    return correlate_exact(h, n)


def fast_convolve(a, b):
    la = len(a)
    lb = len(b)
    if fftconvolve is not None and (la + lb) > 2048:
        out = fftconvolve(a, b)
        return np.asarray(np.real(out), dtype=float)
    return np.convolve(a, b, mode="full")


def smooth_value(c, alpha, x, n, mu):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    s = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(s)
    val = m + float(np.log(np.sum(e))) / alpha
    if mu > 0.0:
        diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
        val += mu * diff * diff
    return float(val)


def softmax_weights(c, alpha):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    s = alpha * (c - m)
    e = np.exp(s)
    ssum = float(np.sum(e))
    if ssum <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ssum):
        return np.full(len(c), 1.0 / float(len(c)))
    return e / ssum


def smooth_full_obj(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = correlate_fast(x, n)
    return smooth_value(c, alpha, x, n, mu)


def weighted_grad_dense(W, x, n):
    W = np.asarray(W, dtype=float)
    if W.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n)
    cs = np.empty(W.size + 1, dtype=float)
    cs[0] = 0.0
    cs[1:] = np.cumsum(W)
    L = cs[n:2 * n] - cs[0:n]
    conv1 = fast_convolve(x, W)
    P1 = conv1[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    conv2 = fast_convolve(W, x[::-1])
    P2 = conv2[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
    return L - P1 - P2


def smooth_full_grad(x, n, alpha, mu):
    c = correlate_fast(x, n)
    w = softmax_weights(c, alpha)
    g = weighted_grad_dense(w, x, n)
    if mu > 0.0:
        diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
        g = g + 2.0 * mu * diff
    return g


def active_uniform_grad(active, x, n):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if len(active) == 0:
        return g
    w = 1.0 / float(len(active))
    off = n - 1
    for t in active:
        k = int(t) - off
        if k >= 0:
            m = n - k
            g[k:n] += w * (1.0 - x[0:m])
            g[0:m] += -w * x[k:n]
        else:
            q = -k
            m = n - q
            g[0:m] += w * (1.0 - x[q:n])
            g[q:n] += -w * x[0:m]
    return g


def active_obj(x, n, active, mu):
    c = correlate_fast(x, n)
    val = float(np.mean(c[active]))
    if mu > 0.0:
        diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
        val += mu * diff * diff
    return val


def active_grad(x, n, active, mu):
    g = active_uniform_grad(active, x, n)
    if mu > 0.0:
        diff = float(np.sum(x)) - 0.5 * float(n)
        g = g + 2.0 * mu * diff
    return g


def select_active(c, top_k, frac_tol, fill):
    M = len(c)
    kk = min(int(top_k), M)
    if kk <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    maxv = float(np.max(c))
    tol = max(1e-12, abs(frac_tol) * max(0.0, maxv))
    cand = np.where(c >= maxv - tol)[0]
    if cand.size == 0:
        return np.argsort(-c)[:kk]
    if cand.size <= kk:
        if fill and cand.size < kk:
            all_top = np.argsort(-c)[:kk]
            if all_top.size > cand.size:
                return all_top
        return cand
    order = np.argsort(-c[cand])
    return cand[order[:kk]]


def lbfgs_step(fun, jac, x0, n, args, maxit, limit):
    x0 = np.clip(np.asarray(x0, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0).copy()
    if maxit <= 0:
        return x0
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    maxfun = max(1, int(maxit * 8))
    options = {
        "maxiter": int(maxit),
        "maxfun": maxfun,
        "ftol": 1e-20,
        "gtol": 1e-14,
        "maxcor": 50,
        "maxls": 30,
    }
    try:
        res = minimize(fun, x0, jac=jac, args=args, method="L-BFGS-B",
                       bounds=bounds, options=options)
        x = res.x if hasattr(res, "x") else x0
    except Exception:
        x = x0
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = x0
    return project_feasible(x, n)


def polish_solution(h, n, deadline, max_seconds):
    t0 = time.time()
    limit = min(float(deadline), t0 + max(0.0, float(max_seconds)))
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    if time.time() >= limit - 5:
        return best_h, best_c

    alphas = (5.0, 20.0, 100.0, 500.0, 2000.0)
    mus = (0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 1.0, 2.0)
    for idx in range(len(alphas)):
        if time.time() >= limit - 10:
            break
        alpha = alphas[idx]
        mu = mus[idx] if idx < len(mus) else 2.0
        rem = limit - time.time()
        scale = 500.0 / float(n)
        if scale < 0.25:
            scale = 0.25
        maxit = int(max(40, min(1500, rem * 1.5 * scale)))
        if maxit < 25:
            break
        x = lbfgs_step(smooth_full_obj, smooth_full_grad, best_h, n,
                       (n, alpha, mu), maxit, limit)
        c = compute_c5(x, n)
        if c < best_c:
            best_c = c
            best_h = x.copy()

    topks = (8, 16, 32)
    for topk in topks:
        for _ in range(2):
            if time.time() >= limit - 8:
                break
            if n <= 0:
                break
            cvec = correlate_exact(best_h, n)
            active = select_active(cvec, topk, 0.002, True)
            if len(active) == 0:
                continue
            rem = limit - time.time()
            scale = 500.0 / float(n)
            if scale < 0.25:
                scale = 0.25
            maxit = int(max(20, min(1000, rem * 1.0 * scale)))
            if maxit < 15:
                break
            x = lbfgs_step(active_obj, active_grad, best_h, n,
                           (n, active, 1.0), maxit, limit)
            c = compute_c5(x, n)
            if c < best_c:
                best_c = c
                best_h = x.copy()
    return best_h, best_c


def make_binary_from_values(vals, n):
    vals = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float)
    if vals.size != n:
        if vals.size == 0:
            vals = np.full(n, 0.5)
        else:
            vals = np.resize(vals, n)
    k = n // 2
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k <= 0:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    if k >= n:
        h[:] = 1.0
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    idx = np.argpartition(vals, k - 1)
    h[idx[-k:]] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_beatty(n, slope):
    t = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    v = np.mod(t * slope, 1.0)
    h = (v < 0.5).astype(float)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_random_binary(n, rng):
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = n // 2
    if k > 0 and k < n:
        idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
        h[idx] = 1.0
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_sinusoid(n, freq, amp, phase=0.0):
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    h = 0.5 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x / float(n) + phase)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def resample_parent(h_old, n):
    old_n = len(h_old)
    if old_n <= 0:
        return project_feasible(np.full(n, 0.5), n)
    if old_n == 1:
        return project_feasible(np.full(n, float(h_old[0])), n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, old_n, old_n, endpoint=False)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, old_n, n, endpoint=False)
    h_ext = np.concatenate((h_old, h_old[:1]))
    return project_feasible(np.interp(x_new, x_old, h_ext), n)


def generate_starts(n, rng, parent):
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        lp = len(parent)
        if lp > 0:
            if lp == n:
                p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=float)
                starts.append(p.copy())
                starts.append(make_binary_from_values(p, n))
                if n > 1:
                    for sh in (1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 11):
                        if sh < n:
                            starts.append(np.roll(p.copy(), sh))
                    starts.append(p[::-1].copy())
            elif n % lp == 0:
                rep = n // lp
                starts.append(np.repeat(parent, rep))
                starts.append(make_binary_from_values(np.repeat(parent, rep), n))
            else:
                r = resample_parent(parent, n)
                starts.append(r)
                starts.append(make_binary_from_values(r, n))

    slopes = (
        0.6180339887498948,
        0.7071067811865476,
        0.4142135623730951,
        0.2928932188134524,
    )
    for s in slopes:
        starts.append(make_beatty(n, s))

    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
        starts.append(make_sinusoid(n, f, 0.45, 0.0))
        if f in (2, 3):
            starts.append(make_sinusoid(n, f, 0.45, np.pi / 4.0))

    for _ in range(4):
        starts.append(make_random_binary(n, rng))

    return starts


def perturb_h(h, n, rng, strength):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
    n_moves = max(10, n // 5)
    if strength <= 0.0:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    for _ in range(n_moves):
        i = int(rng.randint(n))
        j = int(rng.randint(n))
        if i == j:
            continue
        if h[i] > 1e-7:
            amt = float(rng.uniform(0.0, strength))
            if amt > h[i]:
                amt = float(h[i])
            if amt > 0.0 and h[j] + amt <= 1.0:
                h[i] -= amt
                h[j] += amt
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def apply_move_c(c, h, i, j, amt, n):
    cnew = c.copy()
    off = n - 1
    omh = 1.0 - h

    d = -float(amt)
    if i > 0:
        cnew[slice(off + i, i - 1, -1)] += d * omh
    else:
        cnew[slice(off + i, None, -1)] += d * omh
    cnew[slice(off - i, off - i + n, 1)] -= d * h

    d = float(amt)
    if j > 0:
        cnew[slice(off + j, j - 1, -1)] += d * omh
    else:
        cnew[slice(off + j, None, -1)] += d * omh
    cnew[slice(off - j, off - j + n, 1)] -= d * h

    cnew[off + i - j] += amt * amt
    cnew[off + j - i] += amt * amt
    cnew[off] -= 2.0 * amt * amt
    return cnew


def apply_move_h(h, i, j, amt, n):
    hnew = h.copy()
    hnew[i] -= float(amt)
    hnew[j] += float(amt)
    if hnew[i] < 0.0:
        hnew[i] = 0.0
    if hnew[j] > 1.0:
        hnew[j] = 1.0
    return hnew


def local_mass_search(h, n, deadline, rng, max_seconds=60.0):
    t0 = time.time()
    limit = min(float(deadline), t0 + max(0.0, float(max_seconds)))
    cur_h = project_feasible(h, n)
    cur_c = correlate_exact(cur_h, n)
    best_h = cur_h.copy()
    best_craw = float(np.max(cur_c))
    if not np.isfinite(best_craw):
        return project_feasible(cur_h, n), compute_c5(cur_h, n)

    off = n - 1
    move_count = 0
    no_improve = 0
    max_no = 20
    top_side = 10 if n <= 700 else 7
    max_test = 80 if n <= 700 else 50

    while time.time() < limit - 5:
        cmax = float(np.max(cur_c))
        if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
            break

        active = select_active(cur_c, 40, 1e-4, False)
        if len(active) == 0:
            active = select_active(cur_c, 16, 0.0, True)
        if len(active) == 0:
            break
        active_set = set(active.tolist())
        grad = active_uniform_grad(active, cur_h, n)

        down = np.where(cur_h > 1e-7)[0]
        up = np.where(cur_h < 1.0 - 1e-7)[0]
        if len(down) == 0 or len(up) == 0:
            break

        gd = grad[down]
        gu = grad[up]
        top_d = down[np.argsort(-gd)][:top_side]
        top_u = up[np.argsort(gu)][:top_side]

        pairs = []
        seen = set()
        for a in top_d:
            ai = int(a)
            for b in top_u:
                bi = int(b)
                if ai != bi:
                    key = (ai, bi)
                    if key not in seen:
                        seen.add(key)
                        pairs.append(key)
        for _ in range(20):
            ai = int(down[int(rng.randint(len(down)))])
            bi = int(up[int(rng.randint(len(up)))])
            if ai != bi:
                key = (ai, bi)
                if key not in seen:
                    seen.add(key)
                    pairs.append(key)

        scored = []
        for (ai, bi) in pairs:
            score = grad[ai] - grad[bi]
            if score > 1e-10:
                scored.append((-score, ai, bi))
        scored.sort()

        improved = False
        ntest = min(len(scored), max_test)
        for sidx in range(ntest):
            if time.time() > limit:
                break
            _, i, j = scored[sidx]
            maxamt = cur_h[i]
            room = 1.0 - cur_h[j]
            if room < maxamt:
                maxamt = room
            if maxamt < 1e-11:
                continue

            amt_candidates = []
            last_amt = -1.0
            for mult in AMT_MULTS:
                amt = maxamt * mult
                if amt < 1e-12 or amt > maxamt + 1e-15:
                    continue
                if last_amt >= 0.0 and abs(amt - last_amt) <= 1e-16:
                    continue
                amt_candidates.append(float(amt))
                last_amt = float(amt)

            qcnt = 0
            ki = off + i - j
            kj = off + j - i
            if ki in active_set:
                qcnt += 1
            if kj in active_set and kj != ki:
                qcnt += 1
            q = float(qcnt) / float(len(active))
            if off in active_set:
                q -= 2.0 / float(len(active))
            if q > 1e-14:
                L = grad[j] - grad[i]
                if L < -1e-12:
                    aopt = -L / (2.0 * q)
                    if 1e-10 < aopt < maxamt:
                        amt_candidates.append(float(aopt))

            for amt in amt_candidates:
                if amt > maxamt:
                    amt = float(maxamt)
                if amt < 1e-12:
                    continue
                cnew = apply_move_c(cur_c, cur_h, i, j, amt, n)
                newmax = float(np.max(cnew))
                if newmax < best_craw - 1e-12:
                    hnew = apply_move_h(cur_h, i, j, amt, n)
                    if hnew.min() < -1e-9 or hnew.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9:
                        hnew = project_feasible(hnew, n)
                        cnew = correlate_exact(hnew, n)
                        newmax = float(np.max(cnew))
                    if newmax < best_craw:
                        best_craw = newmax
                        best_h = hnew.copy()
                        cur_h = hnew.copy()
                        cur_c = cnew.copy()
                        improved = True
                        move_count += 1
                        if move_count % 25 == 0:
                            cur_h = project_feasible(cur_h, n)
                            cur_c = correlate_exact(cur_h, n)
                            cm = float(np.max(cur_c))
                            if cm < best_craw - 1e-14:
                                best_craw = cm
                                best_h = cur_h.copy()
                        break

            if improved:
                break

        if improved:
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > max_no:
                break

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n)
    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total_budget = max(5.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)
    deadline = t0 + total_budget
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 50:
        main_n = parent.size
    else:
        main_n = 500
    if main_n % 2 != 0:
        main_n += 1
    if main_n < 100:
        main_n = 500

    starts = generate_starts(main_n, rng, parent)
    top1_c = 1e100
    top1_h = None
    top2_c = 1e100
    top2_h = None
    top3_c = 1e100
    top3_h = None

    for h in starts:
        hh = project_feasible(h, main_n)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(hh)):
            continue
        c = compute_c5(hh, main_n)
        if not np.isfinite(c):
            continue
        if c < top1_c:
            top3_c, top3_h = top2_c, top2_h
            top2_c, top2_h = top1_c, top1_h
            top1_c, top1_h = c, hh
        elif c < top2_c:
            top3_c, top3_h = top2_c, top2_h
            top2_c, top2_h = c, hh
        elif c < top3_c:
            top3_c, top3_h = c, hh

    if top1_h is None:
        top1_h = make_random_binary(main_n, rng)
        top1_c = compute_c5(top1_h, main_n)
    overall_h = top1_h
    overall_c = top1_c

    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        t_pol = min(240.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.35))
        h2, c2 = polish_solution(overall_h, main_n, deadline, t_pol)
        if c2 < overall_c:
            overall_h = h2
            overall_c = c2

    if top2_h is not None and top2_c < overall_c + 0.02 and time.time() < deadline - 90:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        t_pol = min(120.0, max(25.0, rem * 0.3))
        h2, c2 = polish_solution(top2_h, main_n, deadline, t_pol)
        if c2 < overall_c:
            overall_h = h2
            overall_c = c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 60:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        t_loc = min(220.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.4))
        h2, c2 = local_mass_search(overall_h, main_n, deadline, rng, t_loc)
        if c2 < overall_c:
            overall_h = h2
            overall_c = c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 80:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        t_pol = min(120.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.5))
        h2, c2 = polish_solution(overall_h, main_n, deadline, t_pol)
        if c2 < overall_c:
            overall_h = h2
            overall_c = c2

    if time.time() < deadline - 35:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        t_loc = min(150.0, max(10.0, rem * 0.7))
        h2, c2 = local_mass_search(overall_h, main_n, deadline, rng, t_loc)
        if c2 < overall_c:
            overall_h = h2
            overall_c = c2

    restarts = 0
    while time.time() < deadline - 130 and restarts < 3:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        strength = 0.05 + 0.03 * float(restarts)
        hp = perturb_h(overall_h, main_n, rng, strength)
        t_pol = min(90.0, max(20.0, rem * 0.35))
        h2, c2 = polish_solution(hp, main_n, deadline, t_pol)
        if c2 < overall_c:
            overall_h = h2
            overall_c = c2
        elif time.time() < deadline - 35:
            rem2 = deadline - time.time()
            t_loc = min(40.0, max(10.0, rem2 * 0.4))
            h3, c3 = local_mass_search(h2, main_n, deadline, rng, t_loc)
            if c3 < overall_c:
                overall_h = h3
                overall_c = c3
        restarts += 1

    alt_n = main_n * 2
    if main_n >= 150 and alt_n <= 1000 and time.time() < deadline - 180:
        starts_alt = generate_starts(alt_n, rng, overall_h)
        best_alt_c = 1e100
        best_alt_h = None
        for ha in starts_alt:
            haa = project_feasible(ha, alt_n)
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(haa)):
                continue
            ca = compute_c5(haa, alt_n)
            if ca < best_alt_c:
                best_alt_c = ca
                best_alt_h = haa
        if best_alt_h is not None and best_alt_c < overall_c + 0.02:
            rem = deadline - time.time()
            t_pol = min(130.0, max(30.0, rem * 0.45))
            ha, ca = polish_solution(best_alt_h, alt_n, deadline, t_pol)
            if ca < overall_c:
                overall_h = ha
                overall_c = ca
            elif time.time() < deadline - 40:
                rem2 = deadline - time.time()
                t_loc = min(50.0, max(10.0, rem2 * 0.45))
                hb, cb = local_mass_search(ha, alt_n, deadline, rng, t_loc)
                if cb < overall_c:
                    overall_h = hb
                    overall_c = cb

    if time.time() < deadline - 8:
        rem = deadline - time.time()
        t_pol = min(30.0, max(4.0, rem - 3.0))
        hn, cn = polish_solution(overall_h, len(overall_h), deadline, t_pol)
        if cn < overall_c:
            overall_h = hn
            overall_c = cn

    fin_n = len(overall_h)
    fin_h = enforce_sum(overall_h, fin_n)
    fin_c = compute_c5(fin_h, fin_n)
    if not np.isfinite(fin_c) or fin_c > 1.0:
        fb = make_random_binary(fin_n, rng)
        fin_h = project_feasible(fb, fin_n)
        fin_c = compute_c5(fin_h, fin_n)

    old_h = np.clip(np.asarray(overall_h, dtype=float), 0.0, 1.0)
    if old_h.size == fin_n:
        old_sum = float(old_h.sum())
        if (abs(old_sum - 0.5 * float(fin_n)) < 1e-8 and
                float(np.min(old_h)) >= -1e-10 and
                float(np.max(old_h)) <= 1.0 + 1e-10):
            old_c = compute_c5(old_h, fin_n)
            if old_c < fin_c:
                fin_h = enforce_sum(old_h, fin_n)
                fin_c = compute_c5(fin_h, fin_n)

    return fin_h, float(fin_c), fin_n
```
